# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-11-12)

## Corpus Check
- 5742 files · ~12,603,143 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 116 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 35, .css 18, .entitlements 11)

## Summary
- 75328 nodes · 220380 edges · 1579 communities (1430 shown, 149 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 9675 edges (avg confidence: 0.87)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `0c27a249`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
